cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(unstructured_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(unstructured unstructured.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(unstructured PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(unstructured m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements
using idx_t = uint64_t;
using val_t = double;

// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)
constexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;

// Material properties for energy transfer
struct Material {
    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient
    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink
};

// Static connectivity information for each element
struct ElementStatic {
    idx_t material_idx;
    idx_t num_connections;
    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements
    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection
};

// Dynamic state for each element
struct ElementDynamic {
    val_t current_energy;
    val_t total_flux;
};

// World state
struct World {
    std::vector<Material> materials;
    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;
};

// Material type IDs
constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;

// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {
    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
    
    // Initialize materials
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material
    
    // Allocate elements
    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);
    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);
    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);
    
    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy
    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;
        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;
        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;
    }
    
    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid
    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {
        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {
            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;
            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];
            
            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)
            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
            
            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {
                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];
                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];
                
                // Check if neighbor is within bounds
                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {
                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;
                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;
                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;
                    elem.num_connections++;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
}

// Compute energy flux between two elements
inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
}

// Run simulation for n_iters iterations
void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
        // Update all elements
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];
            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
            
            // Start with external flow
            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
            
            // Add flux from all connected elements
            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];
                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];
                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);
            }
            
            // Update element state
            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
        }
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
    }
}

// Validate simulation results
bool validateResults(const World& world) {
    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    
    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);
    }
    
    printf("Validation results:\n");
    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
    
    // Check for numerical issues
    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
    
    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows
    }
    
    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);
        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);
        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

    bool validate = false;

    // Parse command line arguments

    printf("Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\n",

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

    printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iter);
    printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elems_per_sec);
    printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Compute hash for verification
    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        std::vector<double> energyData;
        energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());
        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);
        }
        print_results(energyData, "ElementEnergy");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        bool valid = validateResults(world);
        if (!valid) {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;

+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }
-    
-    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;

-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
 }

+void launchUpdate(const World& world, int current) {
+    updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(

+        world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,

-// Run simulation for n_iters iterations
-void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
-    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
+    }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
     }
+    return milliseconds;
+}
+
+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;

+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;

+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }

-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;

-void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
+    }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
     }
+    return milliseconds;
+}
+
+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }

-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;

-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }

-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }

+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+};

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-

-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,

+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }

+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(

+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements
using idx_t = uint64_t;
using val_t = double;

// The square mesh has at most four connections. Store each connection slot
// separately so neighboring GPU threads access consecutive locations.
constexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 4;
constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

// Material properties for energy transfer
struct Material {
    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient
    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink
};

// Dynamic state for each element
struct ElementDynamic {
    val_t current_energy;
    val_t total_flux;
};

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

template <typename T>
struct DeviceBuffer {
    T* data = nullptr;

    DeviceBuffer() = default;
    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }

    void allocate(size_t count) {
        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
                  "cudaMalloc");
    }
};

// All simulation state and connectivity remain on the GPU until reporting.
struct World {
    size_t n_elems = 0;
    unsigned int n_blocks = 0;
    int current = 0;
    DeviceBuffer<uint32_t> connected_idx;
    DeviceBuffer<val_t> connected_flux;
    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> num_connections;
    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> material_idx;
    DeviceBuffer<val_t> energy[2];
    DeviceBuffer<val_t> flux[2];
    DeviceBuffer<ElementDynamic> results;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;
};

__constant__ Material device_materials[3];

// Material type IDs
constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;

__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const uint32_t x = i / root;
        const uint32_t y = i % root;
        uint8_t count = 0;
        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
        if (x + 1 < root) {
            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
        }
        if (x > 0) {
            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
        }
        if (y + 1 < root) {
            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
        }
        if (y > 0) {
            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
        }
        num_connections[i] = count;
        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
        material_idx[i] = material;
        energy[i] = 0.0;
        flux[i] = 0.0;
    }
}

// Build connectivity and initialize the starting state in parallel on CUDA.
void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {
    world.n_elems = size_t(n_elems_root) * n_elems_root;
    int device = 0;
    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
    // Enough resident work to hide memory latency, without oversized grids.
    world.n_blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(std::min(
        (world.n_elems + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE,
        size_t(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32));

    const Material materials[] = {{0.8, 0.0}, {0.8, 0.5}, {0.8, -0.5}};
    checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, materials, sizeof(materials)),
              "copy materials");
    world.connected_idx.allocate(world.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
    world.connected_flux.allocate(world.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
    world.num_connections.allocate(world.n_elems);
    world.material_idx.allocate(world.n_elems);
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        world.energy[i].allocate(world.n_elems);
        world.flux[i].allocate(world.n_elems);
    }
    world.results.allocate(world.n_elems);

    buildMeshKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
        world.n_elems, n_elems_root, world.connected_idx.data,
        world.connected_flux.data, world.num_connections.data,
        world.material_idx.data, world.energy[0].data, world.flux[0].data);
    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "build mesh kernel");
    checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "initialize mesh");
}

__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
            if (j < count) {
                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
            }
        }
        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
    }
}

void launchUpdate(const World& world, int current) {
    updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
        world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
        world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
        world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
        world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
}

// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
                  "create capture stream");
        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
                  "begin graph capture");
        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
                  "instantiate graph");
        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
    }

    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
    int iter = 0;
    if (executable) {
        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
    }
    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
        world.current ^= 1;
    }
    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
    if (executable) {
        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
    }
    return milliseconds;
}

__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
    }
}

void collectResults(World& world) {
    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
              "copy final results");
}

// Validate simulation results
bool validateResults(const World& world) {
    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    
    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+};
