+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }
-    
-    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
 }

+        world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+};

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }
-    
-    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
 }

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}
+
+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }

+                  "cudaMalloc");

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }
-

-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            updateKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+                world.n_elems, world.connected_idx.data, world.connected_flux.data,
+                world.num_connections.data, world.material_idx.data,
+                world.energy[current].data, world.flux[current].data,
+                world.energy[current ^ 1].data, world.flux[current ^ 1].data);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy[world.current].data,
+        world.flux[world.current].data, world.results.data);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), world.results.data,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }
-

-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
 }

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+struct DeviceBuffer {

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+            connected_flux[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = 1.0;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection] * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
+}
+
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
 }

+        }

+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];

 // Material type IDs

-// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
-// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             val_t connection_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }
-    
-    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
-    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
 }

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
 }
 
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
+}

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {

+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             val_t connection_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
 }
 
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
+}

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,re,math
p=Path('unstructured/unstructured.cpp')
original=p.read_text()
configs=[(256,32),(128,32),(512,32),(256,16),(256,64)]
measurements=[]
try:
    for block,cap in configs:
        source=original.replace('constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;',f'constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = {block};').replace('size_t(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32',f'size_t(properties.multiProcessorCount) * {cap}')
        if p.read_text()"'!=source:
            p.write_text(source)
            subprocess.run(['"'cmake','--build','/tmp/unstructured-cuda-build','-j','4'],check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        times=[]
        for n,it in [(1024,500),(2048,200)]:
            output=subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/unstructured-cuda-build/unstructured','-n',str(n),'-i',str(it)],text=True)
            elapsed=float(re.search(r'Computation time: ([\\d.]+) ms',output).group(1))
            times.append(elapsed/it)
        score=math.prod(times)**(1/len(times))
        measurements.append((score,block,cap,times))
        print(f'block={block}, blocks/SM cap={cap}: iteration times {times} ms',flush=True)
finally:

+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+        }

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             val_t connection_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
     }

-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
 }
 
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
+}

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             val_t connection_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
             }
         }
+        next_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
 }
 
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
+}

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+template <typename T>
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    T* data = nullptr;
+
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Device allocation size overflow\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }

-// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh

+__global__ void buildMeshKernel(size_t n_elems, uint32_t root,
+                               uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                               uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const uint32_t x = i / root;
+        const uint32_t y = i % root;
+        uint8_t count = 0;
+        // Append valid neighbors in exactly the reference's order.
+        if (x + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + root;
+        }
+        if (x > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - root;
+        }
+        if (y + 1 < root) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i + 1;
+        }
+        if (y > 0) {
+            connected_idx[size_t(count++) * n_elems + i] = i - 1;
+        }
+        num_connections[i] = count;
+        uint8_t material = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == 0) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == root - 1) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - root) material = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        if (i == n_elems - 1) material = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        material_idx[i] = material;
+        energy[i] = 0.0;
+        flux[i] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+__global__ void updateKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                             const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+                             val_t connection_flux,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+                             const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                             const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+                             val_t* __restrict__ next_flux) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const val_t current_energy = energy[i];
+        const Material mat = device_materials[material_idx[i]];
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+        const unsigned int count = num_connections[i];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t connection = size_t(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const val_t other_energy = energy[connected_idx[connection]];
+                total_flux += (other_energy - current_energy) *
+                              mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;

+        next_flux[i] = flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);

+// Ordered launches provide a global iteration boundary. CUDA graphs amortize
+// launch overhead for long runs, while an even batch preserves buffer parity.
+double runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
+    constexpr int GRAPH_BATCH = 32;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (n_iters >= GRAPH_BATCH) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "create capture stream");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin graph capture");
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < GRAPH_BATCH; ++iter) {
+            const int current = world.current ^ (iter & 1);
+            launchUpdate(world, current, stream);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "end graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(executable, nullptr), "upload graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(nullptr), "finish graph upload");
     }
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&start), "create start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "create stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start), "record start event");
+    int iter = 0;
+    if (executable) {
+        for (; iter <= n_iters - GRAPH_BATCH; iter += GRAPH_BATCH)
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, nullptr), "launch graph");
+    }
+    for (; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+        launchUpdate(world, world.current);
+        world.current ^= 1;
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop), "record stop event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "finish simulation");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start, stop), "elapsed time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(start), "destroy start event");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "destroy stop event");
+    if (executable) {
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable), "destroy executable graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroy graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroy capture stream");
+    }
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+__global__ void collectResultsKernel(size_t n_elems,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
+                                    const val_t* __restrict__ flux,
+                                    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ results) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < n_elems; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        results[i] = {energy[i], flux[i]};
+    }
 }
 
+void collectResults(World& world) {
+    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_elems);
+    // Reuse the inactive state buffer for packing; no extra GPU allocation or
+    // host-side interleaving is needed for the final contiguous download.
+    auto* results = reinterpret_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.state[world.current ^ 1].data);
+    collectResultsKernel<<<world.n_blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        world.n_elems, world.energy(world.current), world.flux(world.current), results);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "collect results kernel");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), results,
+                         world.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "copy final results");
+}
